STEP 53 CALL 1 RETURN 0
COMMAND:
cd /app && cat > packages/core/tests/snapshot.test.ts <<'EOF'
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import {
    createTraitRegistry,
    createWorld,
    diffEntitySnapshots,
    diffWorldSnapshots,
    relation,
    rollbackEntity,
    rollbackWorld,
    snapshotEntity,
    snapshotWorld,
    trait,
    type EntitySnapshot,
    type TraitRegistry,
    type WorldSnapshot,
} from '../src';

const Position = trait({ x: 0, y: 0 });
const Health = trait({ hp: 100 });
const IsPlayer = trait();
const Nested = trait({ info: { name: 'none' } });
const ChildOf = relation();
const Contains = relation({ store: { amount: 0 } });
const TagRel = relation();

function makeRegistry(): TraitRegistry {
    return createTraitRegistry(
        ['position', Position],
        ['health', Health],
        ['isPlayer', IsPlayer],
        ['nested', Nested],
        ['childOf', ChildOf],
        ['contains', Contains],
        ['tagRel', TagRel],
    );
}

describe('createTraitRegistry', () => {
    it('should create a registry from entries', () => {
        const registry = createTraitRegistry(['position', Position], ['childOf', ChildOf]);
        expect(registry.entries.position).toBe(Position);
        expect(registry.entries.childOf).toBe(ChildOf);
    });

    it('should throw on duplicate keys', () => {
        expect(() => createTraitRegistry(['a', Position], ['a', Health])).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw on duplicate traits', () => {
        expect(() => createTraitRegistry(['a', Position], ['b', Position])).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw on duplicate relations', () => {
        expect(() => createTraitRegistry(['a', ChildOf], ['b', ChildOf])).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('snapshotEntity', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    const registry = makeRegistry();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('should snapshot tag traits as true and data traits as deep copies', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }), IsPlayer);
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry);

        expect(snapshot.id).toBe(entity.id());
        expect(snapshot.traits).toEqual({
            position: { x: 1, y: 2 },
            isPlayer: true,
        });
        expect(snapshot.relations).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('should deep copy trait data', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }));
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry);
        (snapshot.traits.position as { x: number }).x = 99;
        expect(entity.get(Position)!.x).toBe(1);
    });

    it('should snapshot relations with and without data', () => {
        const player = world.spawn();
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(player, { amount: 3 }), ChildOf(player), TagRel(player));
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, item, registry);

        expect(snapshot.relations).toEqual({
            contains: [{ targetId: player.id(), data: { amount: 3 } }],
            childOf: [{ targetId: player.id() }],
            tagRel: [{ targetId: player.id() }],
        });
    });

    it('should omit relations when entity has none', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn(Position());
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry);
        expect(snapshot.relations).toBeUndefined();
    });

    it('should throw for destroyed entities', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn();
        entity.destroy();
        expect(() => snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry)).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw for unregistered traits', () => {
        const Unregistered = trait();
        const entity = world.spawn(Unregistered);
        expect(() => snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry)).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw for unregistered relations', () => {
        const UnregisteredRel = relation();
        const target = world.spawn();
        const entity = world.spawn(UnregisteredRel(target));
        expect(() => snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry)).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('snapshotWorld', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    const registry = makeRegistry();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('should exclude the internal world entity', () => {
        const a = world.spawn(Position());
        const b = world.spawn(Position());
        const snapshot = snapshotWorld(world, registry);
        expect(snapshot.entities.map((e) => e.id).sort()).toEqual([a.id(), b.id()].sort());
    });

    it('should return empty entities for an empty world', () => {
        const snapshot = snapshotWorld(world, registry);
        expect(snapshot.entities).toEqual([]);
    });
});

describe('rollbackEntity', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    const registry = makeRegistry();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('should restore traits and relations exactly', () => {
        const player = world.spawn();
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(player, { amount: 3 }), Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }), IsPlayer);
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, item, registry);

        // Mutate state.
        item.remove(IsPlayer);
        item.set(Position, { x: 100, y: 200 });
        item.set(Contains(player), { amount: 42 });
        item.add(Health({ hp: 10 }));

        rollbackEntity(world, item, registry, snapshot);

        const after = snapshotEntity(world, item, registry);
        expect(after).toEqual(snapshot);
    });

    it('should remove traits and relations not in snapshot', () => {
        const player = world.spawn();
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(player, { amount: 1 }), Position());
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, item, registry);

        item.add(Health({ hp: 50 }));
        item.add(ChildOf(player));
        item.add(TagRel(player));

        rollbackEntity(world, item, registry, snapshot);
        expect(item.has(Health)).toBe(false);
        expect(item.has(ChildOf)).toBe(false);
        expect(item.has(TagRel)).toBe(false);
        expect(item.has(Position)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should throw if relation target does not exist', () => {
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(world.spawn(), { amount: 1 }));
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, item, registry);

        // Destroy the target then rollback.
        const targetId = snapshot.relations!.contains[0].targetId;
        const target = world.entities.find((e) => e.id() === targetId)!;
        target.destroy();

        expect(() => rollbackEntity(world, item, registry, snapshot)).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw for destroyed entities', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn();
        const snapshot = snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry);
        entity.destroy();
        expect(() => rollbackEntity(world, entity, registry, snapshot)).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw for unknown registry keys', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn();
        const snapshot: EntitySnapshot = {
            id: entity.id(),
            traits: { notRegistered: true },
        };
        expect(() => rollbackEntity(world, entity, registry, snapshot)).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('rollbackWorld', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    const registry = makeRegistry();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('should fully replace world state and recreate entity IDs', () => {
        const player = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }), IsPlayer);
        const item = world.spawn(Contains(player, { amount: 3 }));
        const checkpoint = snapshotWorld(world, registry);

        // Mutate state heavily.
        world.spawn(Position({ x: 99, y: 99 }));
        player.set(Position, { x: 100, y: 200 });
        item.destroy();

        rollbackWorld(world, registry, checkpoint);

        const after = snapshotWorld(world, registry);
        expect(after).toEqual(checkpoint);

        const ids = after.entities.map((e) => e.id).sort((a, b) => a - b);
        expect(ids).toEqual([player.id(), item.id()].sort((a, b) => a - b));
        expect(world.has(player)).toBe(true);
        expect(world.has(item)).toBe(true);
    });

    it('should throw for unknown registry keys', () => {
        const badCheckpoint: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [{ id: 1, traits: { nope: true } }],
        };
        expect(() => rollbackWorld(world, registry, badCheckpoint)).toThrow(Error);
    });

    it('should throw for dangling relation targets', () => {
        const badCheckpoint: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: {},
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 999 }] },
                },
            ],
        };
        expect(() => rollbackWorld(world, registry, badCheckpoint)).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('diffEntitySnapshots', () => {
    it('should compute added, removed and changed traits sorted', () => {
        const a: EntitySnapshot = {
            id: 1,
            traits: { a: true, b: { v: 1 }, c: true },
        };
        const b: EntitySnapshot = {
            id: 1,
            traits: { b: { v: 2 }, c: true, d: true },
        };
        expect(diffEntitySnapshots(a, b)).toEqual({
            addedTraits: ['d'],
            removedTraits: ['a'],
            changedTraits: ['b'],
        });
    });

    it('should use shallow equality for data', () => {
        const a: EntitySnapshot = {
            id: 1,
            traits: { a: { x: { y: 1 } } },
        };
        const b: EntitySnapshot = {
            id: 1,
            traits: { a: { x: { y: 2 } } },
        };
        // Shallow equality: nested objects differ by reference, so not equal.
        expect(diffEntitySnapshots(a, b).changedTraits).toEqual(['a']);
    });

    it('should throw for null/undefined', () => {
        expect(() => diffEntitySnapshots(null as any, {} as any)).toThrow(Error);
        expect(() => diffEntitySnapshots({} as any, undefined as any)).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('diffWorldSnapshots', () => {
    it('should compute added, removed and changed ids sorted', () => {
        const before: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                { id: 1, traits: { a: true } },
                { id: 2, traits: { a: true } },
                { id: 3, traits: { a: true } },
            ],
        };
        const after: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                { id: 2, traits: { a: false } },
                { id: 3, traits: { a: true } },
                { id: 4, traits: { a: true } },
            ],
        };
        expect(diffWorldSnapshots(before, after)).toEqual({
            added: [4],
            removed: [1],
            changed: [2],
        });
    });

    it('should treat empty relations and missing relations as equal', () => {
        const before: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [{ id: 1, traits: { a: true } }],
        };
        const after: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [{ id: 1, traits: { a: true }, relations: {} }],
        };
        expect(diffWorldSnapshots(before, after)).toEqual({
            added: [],
            removed: [],
            changed: [],
        });
    });

    it('should ignore trait, relation and target ordering', () => {
        const before: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: { a: true, b: true },
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 5 }, { targetId: 6 }] },
                },
            ],
        };
        const after: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: { b: true, a: true },
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 6 }, { targetId: 5 }] },
                },
            ],
        };
        expect(diffWorldSnapshots(before, after)).toEqual({
            added: [],
            removed: [],
            changed: [],
        });
    });

    it('should throw for missing entities array or null', () => {
        expect(() => diffWorldSnapshots(null as any, {} as any)).toThrow(Error);
        expect(() => diffWorldSnapshots({} as any, { entities: [] } as any)).toThrow(Error);
    });
});

describe('convenience methods', () => {
    const world = createWorld();
    const registry = makeRegistry();

    beforeEach(() => {
        world.reset();
    });

    it('should expose world.snapshot and world.rollback', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }));
        const checkpoint = world.snapshot(registry);
        expect(Array.isArray(checkpoint.entities)).toBe(true);

        entity.set(Position, { x: 100, y: 200 });
        world.rollback(registry, checkpoint);
        expect(entity.get(Position)!.x).toBe(1);
    });

    it('should expose entity.snapshot and entity.rollback', () => {
        const entity = world.spawn(Position({ x: 1, y: 2 }), IsPlayer);
        const snapshot = entity.snapshot(registry);
        entity.set(Position, { x: 100, y: 200 });
        entity.remove(IsPlayer);
        entity.rollback(registry, snapshot);
        expect(entity.get(Position)!.x).toBe(1);
        expect(entity.has(IsPlayer)).toBe(true);
    });
});

cd /app && python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
p = Path('packages/core/src/snapshot/snapshot.ts')
s = p.read_text()

# Fix rollbackWorld: validate target 0 as world entity; create all entities first, then populate.
old = '''    // Validate all keys and relation targets up front.
    const checkpointIds = new Set<number>();
    for (const entity of checkpoint.entities) {
        if (typeof entity.id !== 'number') {
            throw new Error('Koota: Invalid checkpoint: entity snapshot has no numeric id.');
        }
        if (checkpointIds.has(entity.id)) {
            throw new Error(`Koota: Invalid checkpoint: duplicate entity id ${entity.id}.`);
        }
        checkpointIds.add(entity.id);

        for (const key of Object.keys(entity.traits)) {
            assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
        }
        const entityRelations = entity.relations ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entityRelations)) {
            assertRegistryRelation(registry, key);
            for (const entry of entityRelations[key]) {
                if (!checkpointIds.has(entry.targetId)) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Koota: Relation "${key}" targets entity ${entry.targetId} which is not present in the checkpoint.`
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Fully replace existing world state.
    world.reset();

    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // Recreate entities using the same IDs as in the checkpoint.
    for (const entitySnapshot of checkpoint.entities) {
        const entity = allocateEntityAt(ctx.entityIndex, entitySnapshot.id);
        ctx.entityTraits.set(entity, new Set());

        // Add traits.
        for (const key of Object.keys(entitySnapshot.traits)) {
            const value = entitySnapshot.traits[key];
            const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;
            if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') {
                addTrait(world, entity, trait);
            } else {
                addTrait(world, entity, [trait, value]);
            }
        }

        // Add relations.
        const entityRelations = entitySnapshot.relations ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entityRelations)) {
            const relation = registry.entries[key] as Relation;
            for (const entry of entityRelations[key]) {
                const target = findAliveEntityById(world, entry.targetId)!;
                if (entry.data !== undefined) {
                    addTrait(world, entity, relation(target, entry.data as Record<string, unknown>));
                } else {
                    addTrait(world, entity, relation(target));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
'''

new = '''    // Validate all keys and relation targets up front.
    const checkpointIds = new Set<number>();
    for (const entity of checkpoint.entities) {
        if (typeof entity.id !== 'number') {
            throw new Error('Koota: Invalid checkpoint: entity snapshot has no numeric id.');
        }
        if (checkpointIds.has(entity.id)) {
            throw new Error(`Koota: Invalid checkpoint: duplicate entity id ${entity.id}.`);
        }
        checkpointIds.add(entity.id);

        for (const key of Object.keys(entity.traits)) {
            assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
        }
        const entityRelations = entity.relations ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entityRelations)) {
            assertRegistryRelation(registry, key);
            for (const entry of entityRelations[key]) {
                // The world entity (id 0) is recreated by reset(), so it is always valid.
                if (!checkpointIds.has(entry.targetId) && entry.targetId !== 0) {
                    throw new Error(
                        `Koota: Relation "${key}" targets entity ${entry.targetId} which is not present in the checkpoint.`
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // Fully replace existing world state.
    world.reset();

    const ctx = world[$internal];

    // First recreate every entity using the same IDs as in the checkpoint.
    const recreated: Entity[] = [];
    for (const entitySnapshot of checkpoint.entities) {
        const entity = allocateEntityAt(ctx.entityIndex, entitySnapshot.id);
        ctx.entityTraits.set(entity, new Set());
        recreated.push(entity);
    }

    // Then populate traits and relations so targets are always available.
    for (let i = 0; i < checkpoint.entities.length; i++) {
        const entitySnapshot = checkpoint.entities[i];
        const entity = recreated[i];

        // Add traits.
        for (const key of Object.keys(entitySnapshot.traits)) {
            const value = entitySnapshot.traits[key];
            const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;
            if (trait[$internal].type === 'tag') {
                addTrait(world, entity, trait);
            } else {
                addTrait(world, entity, [trait, value]);
            }
        }

        // Add relations.
        const entityRelations = entitySnapshot.relations ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entityRelations)) {
            const relation = registry.entries[key] as Relation;
            for (const entry of entityRelations[key]) {
                const target = findAliveEntityById(world, entry.targetId)!;
                if (entry.data !== undefined) {
                    addTrait(world, entity, relation(target, entry.data as Record<string, unknown>));
                } else {
                    addTrait(world, entity, relation(target));
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
'''

if old not in s:
    print("OLD BLOCK NOT FOUND")
else:
    s = s.replace(old, new)
    p.write_text(s)
    print("patched")
PY
OUTPUT:
patched

from pathlib import Path
p = Path('packages/core/tests/snapshot.test.ts')
s = p.read_text()
# Add tests inside rollbackWorld describe, before the final convenience describe
marker = '''    it('should throw for dangling relation targets', () => {
        const badCheckpoint: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: {},
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 999 }] },
                },
            ],
        };
        expect(() => rollbackWorld(world, registry, badCheckpoint)).toThrow(Error);
    });
'''
addition = marker + '''
    it('should handle relations to entities that appear later in the checkpoint', () => {
        // Entity 1 relates to entity 2, but entity 2 is listed after entity 1.
        const checkpoint: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: {},
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 2 }] },
                },
                { id: 2, traits: {} },
            ],
        };
        rollbackWorld(world, registry, checkpoint);
        const after = snapshotWorld(world, registry);
        expect(after.entities.length).toBe(2);
        const source = world.entities.find((e) => e.id() === 1)!;
        const target = world.entities.find((e) => e.id() === 2)!;
        expect(source.targetFor(ChildOf)).toBe(target);
    });

    it('should allow relations targeting the world entity', () => {
        const checkpoint: WorldSnapshot = {
            entities: [
                {
                    id: 1,
                    traits: {},
                    relations: { childOf: [{ targetId: 0 }] },
                },
            ],
        };
        rollbackWorld(world, registry, checkpoint);
        const source = world.entities.find((e) => e.id() === 1)!;
        const worldEntity = world.entities.find((e) => e.id() === 0)!;
        expect(source.targetFor(ChildOf)).toBe(worldEntity);
    });
'''
if marker not in s:
    print("marker not found")
else:
    s = s.replace(marker, addition)
    p.write_text(s)
    print("added")

    57	/**
    58	 * Creates a trait registry mapping string keys to traits and relations.
    59	 * The registry is used to serialize/deserialize entity and world state.
    60	 */
    61	export function createTraitRegistry(...entries: TraitRegistryEntry[]): TraitRegistry {
    62	    const registry: TraitRegistry = {
    63	        entries: {},
    64	        traitKeys: new Map(),
    65	        relationKeys: new Map(),
    66	    };
    67	
    68	    const keys = new Set<string>();
    69	    const traits = new Set<Trait>();
    70	    const relations = new Set<Relation>();
    71	
    72	    for (const [key, value] of entries) {
    73	        if (keys.has(key)) {
    74	            throw new Error(`Koota: Duplicate key "${key}" in trait registry.`);
    75	        }
    76	        keys.add(key);
    77	
    78	        if (isRelation(value)) {
    79	            if (relations.has(value)) {
    80	                throw new Error(`Koota: Duplicate relation in trait registry.`);
    81	            }
    82	            relations.add(value);
    83	            registry.relationKeys.set(value, key);
    84	        } else {
    85	            if (traits.has(value)) {
    86	                throw new Error(`Koota: Duplicate trait in trait registry.`);
    87	            }
    88	            traits.add(value);
    89	            registry.traitKeys.set(value, key);
    90	        }
    91	
    92	        registry.entries[key] = value;
    93	    }
    94	
    95	    return registry;
    96	}
    97	
    98	function deepClone<T>(value: T): T {
    99	    if (value === null || typeof value !== 'object') return value;
   100	    if (typeof structuredClone === 'function') {
   101	        try {
   102	            return structuredClone(value);
   103	        } catch {
   104	            // Fall through to manual clone for non-cloneable values.
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map(deepClone) as unknown as T;
   108	    const result: Record<string, unknown> = {};
   109	    for (const key of Object.keys(value)) {
   110	        result[key] = deepClone((value as Record<string, unknown>)[key]);
   111	    }
   112	    return result as unknown as T;
   113	}
   114	
   115	function assertEntityAlive(world: World, entity: Entity): void {
   116	    if (!isEntityAlive(world[$internal].entityIndex, entity)) {
   117	        throw new Error('Koota: Cannot snapshot/rollback a destroyed entity.');
   118	    }
   119	}
   120	
   121	function assertRegistryTrait(registry: TraitRegistry, key: string): Trait {
   122	    const value = registry.entries[key];
   123	    if (!value) {
   124	        throw new Error(`Koota: Unknown trait key "${key}" in trait registry.`);
   125	    }
   126	    if (isRelation(value)) {
   127	        throw new Error(`Koota: Registry key "${key}" is a relation, not a trait.`);
   128	    }
   129	    return value;
   130	}
   131	
   132	function assertRegistryRelation(registry: TraitRegistry, key: string): Relation {
   133	    const value = registry.entries[key];
   134	    if (!value) {
   135	        throw new Error(`Koota: Unknown relation key "${key}" in trait registry.`);
   136	    }
   137	    if (!isRelation(value)) {
   138	        throw new Error(`Koota: Registry key "${key}" is a trait, not a relation.`);
   139	    }
   140	    return value;
   141	}
   142	
   143	/**
   144	 * Captures a snapshot of a single entity.
   145	 */
   146	export function snapshotEntity(world: World, entity: Entity, registry: TraitRegistry): EntitySnapshot {
   147	    assertEntityAlive(world, entity);
   148	
   149	    const ctx = world[$internal];
   150	    const currentTraits = ctx.entityTraits.get(entity) ?? new Set<Trait>();
   151	
   152	    const traits: Record<string, object | true> = {};
   153	    const relations: Record<string, RelationSnapshotEntry[]> = {};
   154	
   155	    for (const trait of currentTraits) {
   156	        const traitCtx = trait[$internal];
   157	        const relation = traitCtx.relation;
   158	
   159	        if (relation) {
   160	            const key = registry.relationKeys.get(relation);
   161	            if (key === undefined) {
   162	                throw new Error('Koota: Cannot snapshot an unregistered relation.');
   163	            }
   164	
   165	            const entries: RelationSnapshotEntry[] = [];
   166	            const targets = getRelationTargets(world, relation, entity);
   167	            const hasStore = traitCtx.type !== 'tag';
   168	
   169	            for (const target of targets) {
   170	                const entry: RelationSnapshotEntry = { targetId: getEntityId(target) };
   171	                if (hasStore) {
   172	                    const data = getRelationData(world, entity, relation, target);
   173	                    if (data !== undefined) entry.data = deepClone(data) as object;
   174	                }
   175	                entries.push(entry);
   176	            }
   177	
   178	            relations[key] = entries;
   179	        } else {
   180	            const key = registry.traitKeys.get(trait);
   181	            if (key === undefined) {
   182	                throw new Error('Koota: Cannot snapshot an unregistered trait.');
   183	            }
   184	
   185	            if (traitCtx.type === 'tag') {
   186	                traits[key] = true;
   187	            } else {
   188	                traits[key] = deepClone(getTrait(world, entity, trait));
   189	            }
   190	        }
   191	    }
   192	
   193	    const snapshot: EntitySnapshot = {
   194	        id: getEntityId(entity),
   195	        traits,
   196	    };
   197	
   198	    if (Object.keys(relations).length > 0) {
   199	        snapshot.relations = relations;
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    return snapshot;
   203	}
   204	
   205	/**
   206	 * Captures a snapshot of an entire world, excluding the internal world entity.
   207	 */
   208	export function snapshotWorld(world: World, registry: TraitRegistry): WorldSnapshot {
   209	    const worldEntity = world[$internal].worldEntity;
   210	    const entities: EntitySnapshot[] = [];
   211	
   212	    for (const entity of world.entities) {
   213	        if (entity === worldEntity) continue;
   214	        entities.push(snapshotEntity(world, entity, registry));
   215	    }
   216	
   217	    return { entities };
   218	}

# rollbackEntity: normalize snapshot.traits
s = s.replace("""    // Validate all snapshot keys up front.
    for (const key of Object.keys(snapshot.traits)) {
        assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
    }""", """    // Validate all snapshot keys up front.
    const snapshotTraits = snapshot.traits ?? {};
    for (const key of Object.keys(snapshotTraits)) {
        assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
    }""")
s = s.replace("""            const key = registry.traitKeys.get(trait);
            if (key === undefined || !(key in snapshot.traits)) {
                removeTrait(world, entity, trait);
            }""", """            const key = registry.traitKeys.get(trait);
            if (key === undefined || !(key in snapshotTraits)) {
                removeTrait(world, entity, trait);
            }""")
s = s.replace("""    // Add/update traits to exactly match the snapshot.
    for (const key of Object.keys(snapshot.traits)) {
        const value = snapshot.traits[key];
        const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;""", """    // Add/update traits to exactly match the snapshot.
    for (const key of Object.keys(snapshotTraits)) {
        const value = snapshotTraits[key];
        const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;""")

# rollbackWorld validation: normalize entity.traits
s = s.replace("""        for (const key of Object.keys(entity.traits)) {
            assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
        }""", """        const entityTraits = entity.traits ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entityTraits)) {
            assertRegistryTrait(registry, key);
        }""")

# rollbackWorld population: normalize traits
s = s.replace("""        // Add traits.
        for (const key of Object.keys(entitySnapshot.traits)) {
            const value = entitySnapshot.traits[key];
            const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;""", """        // Add traits.
        const entitySnapshotTraits = entitySnapshot.traits ?? {};
        for (const key of Object.keys(entitySnapshotTraits)) {
            const value = entitySnapshotTraits[key];
            const trait = registry.entries[key] as Trait;""")

# entitySnapshotsEqual: normalize traits
s = s.replace("""    if (a.id !== b.id) return false;
    if (!shallowEqual(a.traits, b.traits)) return false;""", """    if (a.id !== b.id) return false;
    if (!shallowEqual(a.traits ?? {}, b.traits ?? {})) return false;""")

p.write_text(s)
print("done")

     return entity;

+/**
+ * Allocates an entity with a specific ID (generation 0).
+ * Used by the snapshot/rollback system to recreate entities
+ * using the same IDs they had when a checkpoint was captured.
+ * @param index - The EntityIndex to add to.
+ * @param id - The entity ID to allocate.
+ * @returns The packed entity.
+ */
+export const allocateEntityAt = (index: EntityIndex, id: number): Entity => {
+    const sparseIndex = index.sparse[id];
+    if (sparseIndex !== undefined && sparseIndex < index.aliveCount) {
+        throw new Error(`Koota: Entity with ID ${id} already exists.`);
+    }
+
+    const entity = packEntity(index.worldId, 0, id);
+    const denseIndex = index.aliveCount;
+    index.dense[denseIndex] = entity;
+    index.sparse[id] = denseIndex;
+    index.aliveCount++;
+    if (id >= index.maxId) index.maxId = id + 1;
+
+    return entity;
+};
+
 /**
  * Removes an entity ID from the index.
  * @param index - The EntityIndex to remove from.
